    update
        inventory i1_0 
    set
        quantity=(coalesce(i1_0.quantity, 0)+?),
        reserved_quantity=(coalesce(i1_0.reserved_quantity, 0)+?),
        available_quantity=((coalesce(i1_0.quantity, 0)+?)-(coalesce(i1_0.reserved_quantity, 0)+?)),
        version=(i1_0.version+1),
        updated_at=? 
    where
        i1_0.inventory_id=? 
        and i1_0.deleted_yn='N' 
        and (
            ?=true 
            or coalesce(i1_0.inventory_status, 'available')<>'quarantined'
        ) 
        and (
            coalesce(i1_0.quantity, 0)+?
        )>=0 
        and (
            coalesce(i1_0.reserved_quantity, 0)+?
        )>=0 
        and (
            coalesce(i1_0.reserved_quantity, 0)+?
        )<=(
            coalesce(i1_0.quantity, 0)+?
        )

        inventory i1_0 
    set
        quantity=(coalesce(i1_0.quantity, 0)+?),
        reserved_quantity=(coalesce(i1_0.reserved_quantity, 0)+?),
        available_quantity=((coalesce(i1_0.quantity, 0)+?)-(coalesce(i1_0.reserved_quantity, 0)+?)),
        version=(i1_0.version+1),
        updated_at=? 
    where
        i1_0.inventory_id=?

        and (
            ?=true 
            or coalesce(i1_0.inventory_status, 'available')<>'quarantined'
        )

            coalesce(i1_0.quantity, 0)+?
        )>=0 
        and (
            coalesce(i1_0.reserved_quantity, 0)+?
        )>=0 
        and (
            coalesce(i1_0.reserved_quantity, 0)+?
        )<=(
            coalesce(i1_0.quantity, 0)+?

        )>=0
